Method: 通过信息论证明，对凸机器学习模型，任何仅依赖查询模型行为信号的通用审计方案在识别未充分遗忘模型时必然泄露保留集的成员信息；并在凸模型上进行实证验证，同时在非凸模型上进一步实验证明该隐私-审计张力仍然存在。

Result: 1) 理论上证明了凸模型下行为信号审计存在隐私-审计权衡；2) 凸模型的实验结果强有力地支持该理论结果；3) 非凸模型上的实验表明该隐私-审计张力依然存在。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明在更现实的审计者威胁模型下需要更谨慎地考虑隐私-审计张力，为设计隐私保护的MU审计方案提供理论基础，呼吁对MU流程中审计方案的设计进行更严格的审查。

Abstract: The removal of learned data from Machine Learning models through Machine Unlearning (MU) has been widely studied; however, there has yet to be an agreed-upon scheme for auditing MU. Existing work has shown that a dishonest model owner can falsify evidence to avoid executing MU, while curious auditors (and adversaries) can infer the privacy-sensitive properties of the model and its training data even with limited access. Yet auditing of MU under mutual distrust between the model owner and the auditor remains unexplored. We provide an information-theoretic proof for this scenario: for convex ML models, a generic audit scheme that relies solely on querying the model for \textit{behavioral} signals cannot identify insufficiently unlearned models without revealing membership information of the retained set. Therefore, auditing MU under the assumption of a dishonest model owner and an honest-but-curious auditor faces an inherent privacy-audit tradeoff. Our empirical results on convex models strongly supports this result, while further experiments demonstrate that this privacy-audit tension persists in non-convex models. Our results call for a more careful consideration of the privacy-audit tension under a realistic auditor threat model, and serve as a foundation for more scrutiny of designs of privacy-preserving audit schemes for the MU pipeline. We also release our code implementation at https://github.com/LiouTang/Behavioral-Unlearn-Audit.

</details>


### [76] [Code Correctness Signals in LLM Hidden States: Pre-Generation Probing and Repair Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14530)
*Carlo Di Cicco*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究了Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507模型隐藏状态中代码正确性的可读性，发现：1）首次尝试代码的正确性可从提示最终隐藏状态线性解码（AUC 0.931），即使去除提示长度影响仍达0.911；2）修复失败尝试时隐藏状态的变化方向虽统计显著，但在条件残差化处理后消失，表明该方向是修复上下文驱动的相关性而非独立的修复理解特征。论文贡献兼具方法论和实证性：诚实地报告了正面和负面结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型在生成代码之前，其隐藏状态中是否编码了代码正确性的信息，以及模型在修复失败代码时隐藏状态的变化是否反映修复成功与否。

Method: 使用残差化（residualization）作为混杂因素控制工具，在444个LiveCodeBench任务上对Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507进行分析。第一项研究从提示最终隐藏状态线性解码代码正确性，第二项研究分析修复前后隐藏状态变化方向的统计显著性，并通过条件残差化检验其是否独立于修复上下文的协变量。

Result: 1）代码正确性线性解码AUC达0.931±0.008，去除提示长度影响后仍达0.911±0.010，远超提示长度基线0.754±0.014。2）修复方向在幅度和分割半测试中均显著，但条件残差化后不再显著，表明该方向是修复上下文协变量驱动的相关特征而非独立的修复理解特征。

Conclusion: 模型隐藏状态在生成前就可解码代码正确性（正面结果），但修复过程中隐藏状态的变化方向并非独立的修复理解特征，而是修复上下文的副产品（负面结果）。残差化是揭示混杂因素影响的有效工具。

Abstract: Large language models encode rich information in their hidden states. This work asks whether code correctness is legible in the hidden states of Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, before it generates and as it repairs a failed attempt, studied on 444 LiveCodeBench tasks. It reports two findings connected by a single confound-control tool: residualization. First, the correctness of the model's first-attempt code is linearly decodable from the prompt-final hidden state, with a leakage-free held-out AUC of 0.931 +/- 0.008 across 50 outer splits. After the linear effect of prompt length is removed from each hidden state dimension, the probe still reaches 0.911 +/- 0.010, well above a prompt-length baseline of 0.754 +/- 0.014. Second, on 236 cleaned cases where the model attempts to repair a failed first attempt, the hidden state shift from the failing attempt to its repair carries a statistically detectable contrastive direction, significant on both a magnitude and a split-half test against label-shuffled nulls. This direction does not survive a conditional residualization against repair-context covariates that differ between successful and failed repairs, marking it as a correlate of repair success driven by the repair context rather than an isolated repair-comprehension feature. The probe layer is selected by nested cross-validation, and the same residualization approach that upholds the pre-generation correctness result overturns the repair-direction interpretation. The contribution is as much methodological as empirical: a diagnostic honest enough to report a negative result alongside a positive one.

</details>


### [77] [The Risk Shadow of Principal Component Analysis: When 99.9999% Variance Preservation Causes Catastrophic Decision Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14533)
*Hamidou Tembine*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明PCA在降维时会完全抹去罕见灾难事件的所有信号（即使保留>99.9999%方差），并提出了两种保留尾部风险信息的替代方法：ExPCA和TP-PCA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PCA以方差最大化为目标，但方差最大化与尾部风险感知之间存在根本性不匹配，导致罕见高风险事件的信息在降维中被完全消除。

Method: 提出Expectile PCA (ExPCA)和Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA)，通过重新加权数据协方差矩阵来偏向高影响事件，并给出理论证明ExPCA在保留罕见事件信息上严格优于PCA。

Result: 理论证明存在"Risk Shadow"现象（PCA保留>99.9999%方差但完全抹去罕见事件信号）；在合成数据和真实信用卡欺诈检测基准上验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 在高风险决策中，基于方差的降维方法需要从根本上重新审视，应转向对尾部风险敏感的降维方法。

Abstract: Principal Component Analysis (PCA) preserves variance, not the information needed to detect rare catastrophic events. This paper proves the existence of a {\it Risk Shadow}: PCA can retain over 99.9999 percent of total variance while completely erasing all signal about rare, high-impact failures. When this happens, even the best possible classifier operating on the PCA representation reduces to a constant predictor. The root cause is a fundamental mismatch between variance maximization and tail risk awareness. To break the shadow, we introduce Expectile PCA (ExPCA) and Tail-Preserving PCA (TP-PCA), two methods that reweight the data covariance toward high-impact events. We prove theoretically that ExPCA strictly outperforms PCA in retaining rare-event information, and we validate our claims on synthetic data and a real-world credit card fraud detection benchmark. Our results call for a fundamental rethinking of variance-based dimensionality reduction in high-stakes decisions.

</details>


### [78] [Provably Safe, Yet Scalable Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14536)
*Kai S. Yun,Zeyang Li,Navid Azizan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PS2-RL框架通过两阶段架构——先学习备份策略构建隐式控制不变集，再通过可微投影层训练RL策略——在保持可证明安全性的同时实现了可扩展性，在高达10维状态空间的机器人控制任务上优于先前方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全RL方法存在两难：软约束方法经验成功但缺乏正式安全保证；严格保证的方法依赖显式证书函数，需要直接合成和验证控制不变集，该方法随状态维度扩展性差且产生过度保守行为。

Method: 提出PS2-RL框架，两阶段架构：第一阶段利用提出的安全到达价值函数训练备份策略，通过前向积分系统动力学隐式构建控制不变集；第二阶段通过可微投影层端到端训练RL策略，严格强制执行由备份策略诱导的安全保证。

Result: 在高达10维状态空间的机器人控制任务上进行了评估，该场景下先前可证明安全的RL方法难以应对或不切实际。

Conclusion: PS2-RL框架实现了可证明安全性与可扩展性的兼顾，不限制底层RL算法，可插入任何现有训练流程，为安全强化学习提供了一种兼具理论保证和实际可行性的新范式。

Abstract: Safe reinforcement learning (RL) aims to learn policies that optimize rewards while satisfying constraints. Predominant approaches rely on soft-constrained policy optimization, which has achieved empirical success but does not provide formal safety guarantees for the learned policy. In contrast, methods with strict guarantees typically rely on explicit certificate functions, whose construction requires the direct synthesis and verification of control-invariant sets, a process that scales poorly with state dimension and often yields overly conservative behavior. In this paper, we present the Provably Safe, yet Scalable RL (PS2-RL) framework, a novel two-phase architecture for learning provably safe policies in a scalable manner, designed to overcome the key bottlenecks of prior methods. Rather than explicitly computing invariant sets, PS2-RL leverages a learned backup policy to forward-integrate the system dynamics, generating an implicit control-invariant set online. In the first phase, the backup policy is trained with our proposed safe-arrival value function, which characterizes the optimal backup policy for invariant-set construction. In the second phase, an RL policy is trained end-to-end through a differentiable projection layer that strictly enforces the safety guarantees induced by the learned backup policy. By maximizing the volume of the implicit control-invariant set in the first phase, the resulting PS2 policy from the second phase is performant and scalable, while maintaining provable safety. Crucially, PS2-RL imposes no restrictions on the underlying RL algorithm and can be plugged into any existing training pipeline. We establish theoretical guarantees for the proposed framework and evaluate it on robotic control tasks with state dimensions up to 10, a regime in which prior provably safe RL methods struggle or become impractical.

</details>


### [79] [Zero-shot generalization of transformer neural operators to larger domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14597)
*Armand de Villeroché,Sibo Cheng,Vincent Le Guen,Marc Bocquet,Rem-Sophia Mouradi,Patrick Armand,Alban Farchi,Patrick Massin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过可分解的注意力偏置实现空间局部性和平移等变性的方法，使基于Transformer的神经算子能够零样本泛化到更大尺度的空间域上，在多个PDE基准和3D工业大气流动应用中取得了显著效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Transformer的神经算子隐式假设固定域大小，限制了其在推理时泛化到更大空间域的能力。本文旨在解决域扩展（zero-shot推理到比训练时更大的空间域）问题。

Method: 在注意力logits计算中引入可分解的偏置来实现空间局部性，该偏置保持可分解为查询-键内积形式，兼容优化的注意力核；结合旋转位置编码（RoPE），在不改变Transformer架构的前提下实现可控空间支持的表达性嵌入。

Result: 在多个PDE基准测试和一个3D工业大气流动应用中，该方法显著提升了向更大域进行零样本泛化的性能。

Conclusion: 通过引入可分解的局部性偏置，无需改变Transformer架构即可实现向更大空间域的零样本泛化，验证了空间局部性和平移等变性在此设置中的关键作用。

Abstract: Transformer-based neural operators have shown remarkable performance for approximating solution operators of partial differential equations on complex geometries. However, existing approaches implicitly assume a fixed domain size, which limits their ability to generalize at inference. In this work, we investigate domain extension, namely zero-shot inference on spatial domains that are significantly larger than those encountered during training. We argue that this setting fundamentally requires spatial locality and translation equivariance. We propose to implement this locality via a decomposable bias in the attention logits computation, enabling finely controllable locality while remaining fully decomposable into query-key inner products and directly compatible with optimized attention kernels. Combined with rotary positional embeddings, it enables expressive embeddings with controllable spatial support without altering the transformer architecture. We empirically show that our approach substantially improves zero-shot generalization to larger domains across two PDE benchmarks and a 3D industrial atmospheric flow application. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/cerea-daml/domain-extension.

</details>


### [80] [Realizing Native INT8 Compute for Diffusion Transformers on Consumer GPUs: A Fused INT8 GEMM Kernel for Ideogram 4.0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14598)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现当前扩散Transformer的INT8后训练量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际并未使用INT8张量核心，而是反复量化-反量化回bf16执行矩阵乘法，导致速度反而不如FP8/NF4。作者通过一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM核（int8×int8→int32，在Ampere张量核心上运行，支持per-token×per-channel反量化和bias融合，按GEMM形状自动调优）替换Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer线性层中的dequantize-to-bf16路径，实现了2.8-4.2x的逐GEMM加速，端到端768px加速~1.1x（9-10%），1024px单卡RTX 3090上156.5秒，优于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒），且无质量损失。但该优化仅限于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100和B200上反而不如其原生bf16/FP8路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散Transformer的INT8后训练量化在消费级Ampere GPU上实际比FP8和NF4更慢，经追溯发现是软件实现问题：所谓的"INT8"前向计算只是将权重和激活量化后立即反量化回bf16再执行bf16矩阵乘法，从未真正使用GPU的INT8张量核心，硬件的计算优势完全没有被利用。

Method: 设计一个融合的Triton INT8 GEMM核，在Ampere张量核心上执行int8×int8→int32的矩阵乘法，将per-token×per-channel反量化和bias融合到epilogue中，并按GEMM形状自动调优。将该核直接插入Ideogram 4.0扩散Transformer的线性层，替换原有的dequantize-to-bf16路径。

Result: 该核与torch._int_mm逐位精确，反量化输出与参考相比余弦相似度1.0且无NaN，逐GEMM加速2.8-4.2x。端到端768px获得~1.1x（~9-10%）加速；1024px单卡RTX 3090上156.5秒，快于NF4（164.5秒）和FP8（172.9秒），且无质量损失（PickScore/CLIPScore）。INT8从最慢变种变为最快，1024px实现单GPU可行。

Conclusion: 主要速度目标（超过FP8约9.5%）轻松达成；NF4优势（约4.9%，单次运行n=4）在运行间方差范围内，最好视为一致达到扩展目标。但该优化仅限于消费级Ampere GPU，在A100和B200上该核反而不如这些卡的原生bf16/FP8路径。

Abstract: Post-training INT8 (W8A8) quantization of diffusion transformers is widely deployed as a speed optimization, yet on consumer Ampere GPUs it is frequently slower than the FP8 and NF4 alternatives it is meant to beat. We trace this to a software artifact: the production "INT8" forward quantizes weights and activations only to immediately dequantize them back to bf16 and run a bf16 matrix multiply, never engaging the GPU's INT8 tensor cores, so the hardware's compute advantage is left entirely unrealized. We close this gap with a single fused Triton INT8 GEMM (int8xint8->int32 on Ampere tensor cores, with per-token x per-channel dequantization and bias folded into the epilogue, autotuned per GEMM shape) dropped into the Ideogram 4.0 diffusion transformer's linear layers in place of the dequantize-to-bf16 path. In the kernel, the int8xint8->int32 accumulation is bit-exact against torch._int_mm and the dequantized output matches the reference at cosine similarity 1.0 with no NaNs, running 2.8-4.2x faster than bf16 per GEMM. End to end it delivers a ~1.1x (~9-10%) speedup at 768px, and at 1024px it generates an image in 156.5 s on a single RTX 3090, faster than the single-card NF4 (164.5 s) and FP8 (172.9 s) baselines, at no measurable quality cost on these point estimates (PickScore/CLIPScore). INT8 thus goes from the slowest variant to the fastest, and 1024px becomes single-GPU feasible. The primary speed criterion (beat FP8, by ~9.5%) is comfortably met; the NF4 margin (~4.9%, single-run n=4) is within run-to-run variance we did not quantify and is best read as consistent with meeting the stretch target. We close with an honest deployment map: the win is specific to consumer Ampere, and on A100 and B200 the same kernel loses to those cards' fast native bf16/FP8 paths.

</details>


### [81] [A Statistical and Machine Learning Framework for Operational Threshold Detection and Deployable Dispatch Controller Development in Hydrogen Multi-Energy Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14601)
*Shadi Heenatigala,Hasanika Samarasinghe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文利用一年高分辨率运行数据，通过统计和机器学习框架对氢基多能源系统(H-MES)进行表征。统计分析揭示了由可再生能源盈余驱动的二元运行模式，太阳能辐照度解释了制氢量45.7%的秩方差；随机森林分析发现风力输出在预测重要性中排名第一，尽管其双变量相关性较弱(r=0.167)，揭示了参数方法无法捕捉的非线性动态。序列模型利用强24小时自相关(r=0.845)进行运行预测，强化学习智能体优化了氢能收益调度。核心贡献在于证明了统计与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有氢基多能源系统缺乏基于真实高分辨率运行数据的统计与机器学习联合分析，传统参数化方法难以捕捉系统非线性动态和运行模式。

Method: 采用统计分析方法（秩方差分析、多重回归）与机器学习方法（随机森林、序列模型、强化学习）相结合的框架，基于一年高分辨率运行数据对H-MES进行建模与预测。

Result: 太阳能辐照度解释45.7%的秩方差；电解槽功率是主导线性预测因子；随机森林中风电预测重要性排名第一(r=0.167)；24小时自相关系数为0.845；强化学习成功优化氢能收益调度。

Conclusion: 统计方法与机器学习方法在H-MES建模与控制中具有互补性，需结合使用才能全面表征系统运行特性。

Abstract: This study presents a statistical and machine learning framework for characterizing a hydrogen-based multi-energy system (H-MES) using one year of high-resolution operational data. Statistical analysis revealed a binary operation driven by renewable surplus, with solar irradiance explaining 45.7% of rank-based variance in hydrogen production, a large effect by conventional standards. Only high-irradiance periods triggered meaningful electrolyzer engagement, while electricity demand exerted a weaker inverse suppression effect ($ε^2 = 0.126$). Multiple regression confirmed electrolyzer power as the dominant linear predictor, with a synergistic solar-wind interaction. Notably, Random Forest analysis ranked wind output first in predictive importance despite its weak bivariate correlation (r = 0.167), revealing non-linear dynamics invisible to parametric methods. A sequence model exploited strong 24-hour autocorrelation (r = 0.845) for operational forecasting, while a reinforcement learning agent optimized hydrogen revenue dispatch. The core contribution is demonstrating that statistical and machine learning approaches are complementary for H-MES modeling and control.

</details>


### [82] [Neither Parallel Nor Sequential: How DiffusionGemma Actually Commits Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14620)
*Ali Asaria,Tony Salomone,Deep Gandhi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过钩子采样器测量 DiffusionGemma 26B 模型的提交顺序，发现其解码既非并行也非块自回归，而是呈现部分从左到右的提交偏差，且这种偏差的强度完全取决于分析粒度。模型以大批量同时提交，批次内顺序实际上是未定义的。行为具有任务依赖性：结构化JSON的顺序是任意的，数学推理中位置置信度跟踪正确性，但事实回忆中无信号。主要贡献是方法论上的：诚实测量解码顺序需要处理多种混淆因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放扩散语言模型被宣传为并行、非自回归解码器，但实际提交令牌的顺序几乎从未被测量过，这导致对其解码行为的理解存在空白。

Method: 在 DiffusionGemma 26B 模型的采样器中插入钩子，记录每个画布位置提交的时间、顺序和置信度，在 686 个提示、六种场景的探测集上进行测量分析。

Result: 解码呈现部分从左到右偏差，但强度取决于分析粒度；模型以大批量同时提交令牌，批次内顺序未定义；行为具有任务依赖性和领域特异性。

Conclusion: 中心贡献是方法论层面：测量解码顺序必须处理尾随EOS填充、域内混杂、提交非单调性、块大小敏感性和大批量提交平局等问题，否则会人为制造不存在的解码顺序结果。

Abstract: Open diffusion language models are marketed as parallel, non-autoregressive decoders, yet the order in which a shipped checkpoint actually commits its tokens is almost never measured. We instrument DiffusionGemma 26B, a masked discrete-diffusion mixture-of-experts model built on Gemma 4, hooking its sampler's accept step to record which canvas positions commit, when, and at what confidence. Across a 686-prompt, six-regime probe suite we find that its decoding is neither parallel nor block-autoregressive: it follows a partial left-to-right commit bias whose apparent strength depends almost entirely on the granularity at which you look. Order is weak token by token and strengthens smoothly as the analysis is coarsened, so the model's "block size" turns out to be an artifact of the measuring ruler rather than the architecture. The model commits in large simultaneous batches, leaving much of the within-batch order genuinely undefined rather than merely unobserved. The behaviour is regime-dependent: structured JSON is committed in essentially arbitrary order, and a position's commit confidence tracks correctness on mathematical reasoning but carries no signal on factual recall. Commitment is aggressive, finishing in a short late burst well inside the step budget, while task accuracy matches the model's autoregressive Gemma-4 sibling. Beyond these findings, our central contribution is methodological: measuring decoding order honestly demands handling trailing-EOS padding, within-regime confounding, commit non-monotonicity, block-size sensitivity, and large commit-batch ties, each of which can otherwise manufacture a decoding-order result that is not really there.

</details>


### [83] [Graph Diffusion Residuals for Control-Function Instrumental Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14636)
*Rui Wu,Zongyuan Chen,Hong Xie,Defu Lian,Enhong Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应各向异性仪器热流（A-IHF），一种用于灵活控制函数的确定性图扩散残差提取器。A-IHF将处理变量视为第一阶段特征图上的信号，通过导引扩散检测处理跳跃、衰减跨跳跃电导，并用稀疏图预解式计算生成的控制。观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤。实验表明，在54个合成基准单元中，A-IHF系列在32个单元中击败了最佳非A-IHF基线，其性能在图捕捉分段光滑第一阶段结构时最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的控制函数工具变量估计器需要第一阶段残差而非仅仅是第一阶段预测。高容量第一阶段可能插值处理变量，导致为结果方程留下的残差信息过少。现有方法在灵活控制函数中缺乏有效的残差提取机制。

Method: 提出自适应各向异性仪器热流（A-IHF），一种确定性的图扩散残差提取器。将处理变量视为第一阶段特征图上的信号，通过导引扩散检测大的处理跳跃，衰减跨跳跃的电导，利用稀疏图预解式计算生成控制。观测选择规则仅使用(Z,X)，结合图广义交叉验证、粗糙度、残差化处理相关性和图可容许性过滤。

Result: 在54个合成基准单元中，经守卫观测的A-IHF具有最低的平均结构响应MSE；A-IHF系列在32个单元中击败了最佳非A-IHF基线。在图捕捉分段光滑第一阶段结构时性能最强。误差分解为结构泄漏、残差衰减和残差化处理变异，得到有限样本界、图可容许性率和有限路径选择校准。

Conclusion: A-IHF是一种有效的控制函数残差提取方法，尤其适用于第一阶段具有分段光滑几何结构的场景。其理论分析提供了有限样本保证和选择校准，实验验证了其在多种基线中的优越性能。

Abstract: Control-function instrumental variable estimators need a first-stage residual, not merely a first-stage prediction. High-capacity first stages can interpolate treatment and leave too little residual information for the outcome equation. We study Adaptive Anisotropic Instrumental Heat Flow (A-IHF), a deterministic graph-diffusion residual extractor for flexible control functions. A-IHF treats treatment as a signal on a graph of first-stage features, uses pilot diffusion to detect large treatment jumps, attenuates conductance across those jumps, and computes the generated control with a sparse graph resolvent. Its observational selection rule uses only $(Z,X)$, combining graph generalized cross-validation, roughness, residualized-treatment relevance, and graph-admissibility filtering. The analysis decomposes error into structural leakage, residual attenuation, and residualized treatment variation, yielding finite-sample bounds, graph-admissibility rates under latent piecewise-smooth geometry, and finite-path selection calibration. Across 54 synthetic benchmark cells with tuned graph, kernel, tree, boosting, series, and neural control-function baselines, guarded observational A-IHF has the lowest average structural-response MSE; the A-IHF family beats the best non-A-IHF baseline in 32 cells. Performance is strongest when the graph captures piecewise-smooth first-stage structure.

</details>


### [84] [Online Convex Optimization with Sublinear Noisy Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14640)
*Simone Di Gregorio,Anupam Gupta,Stefano Leonardi,Matteo Russo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在线凸优化（OCO）中，通过有限次（k≤T）含噪成对比较查询（pairwise probes）来降低遗憾界。主要结果表明，即使采用次线性且带噪声的探针预算，也能显著改善最坏情况下的遗憾，得到了关于T、k和噪声参数δ的紧致（对数因子内）遗憾界O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一并推广了两条研究路线：专家设定中的次线性最优专家查询，以及OCO中每轮可用的成对（基于比较）反馈。旨在量化有限次含噪成对查询对OCO遗憾界的提升效果。

Method: 提出统一的探针模型（probing model），结合方差缩减效应量化探针收益，并对连续指数权重（Continuous Exponential Weights）进行二阶（基于方差）分析。

Result: 得到了紧致（对数因子内）的遗憾界O(min{√(dT ln T), dT ln T/(k|1-2δ|)})，且当应用于有限集K的d个专家预测时，所有参数（包括d）的界完全紧致。

Conclusion: 即使次线性且带噪声的成对查询预算也能在完整反馈OCO中改善最坏情况遗憾，且遗憾性能随噪声参数δ向1/2（即随机猜测）平滑退化。

Abstract: We study Online Convex Optimization (OCO) over a convex set $K\subseteq \mathbb R^d$, where in each round $t$ the learner selects $x_t\in K$ and then observes a convex loss $f_t:K\to[0,1]$, with the goal of minimizing regret to the best fixed decision in hindsight. We introduce a unified probing model that generalizes two recent lines of work: sublinear best-expert queries in the experts setting, and pairwise (comparison-based) feedback available every round in OCO. In our framework, the learner has a budget of $k\le T$ pairwise probes; on a probed round it may query two points and learn which one has smaller loss.
  Our main result shows that even a sublinear and noisy probe budget can provably improve worst-case regret in the full feedback OCO regime. With $k$ $δ$-noisy pairwise probes, we obtain: $ \text{Reg}_T \le O\left(\min\left\{\sqrt{dT\ln T},\; \frac{dT\ln T}{k|1-2δ|}\right\}\right) $, which is tight (up to logarithmic factors in $T$) across $T$, $k$ and $δ$. Specifically regarding the noise parameter $δ\in [0,1]$, the regret guarantee smoothly degrades as the oracle response approaches a coin flip, i.e., $δ$ is close to $\frac{1}{2}$. When applying the same techniques to a finite $K$ for the prediction with $d$ experts setting, the resulting rates are instead completely tight in all parameters, including $d$.
  Our analysis gives a streamlined treatment of pairwise probing in OCO by quantifying the benefit of probing via a variance reduction effect, combined with a second-order (variance-based) analysis of Continuous Exponential Weights.

</details>


### [85] [Which Directions Matter? Sparse Design for Affine Robust Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14648)
*Pedro Chumpitaz-Flores,My Duong,Juan S. Borrero,Kaixun Hua*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究在有限字典和预算约束下如何选择不确定性方向子集来构建原子不确定性集，提出基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，证明该目标是单调子模函数，支持贪心算法并具有(1-1/e)近似保证，同时提供损失界证书和半径校准规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒机器学习和优化中不确定性模型的选择至关重要，现有方法缺乏对有限字典和预算约束下应覆盖哪些不确定性方向的系统研究。

Method: 基于覆盖目标的数据驱动选择规则，利用原子不确定性集的闭式支撑函数；证明目标的单调子模性以支持贪心算法；提供损失界证书和半径校准规则以实现样本外控制。

Result: 证明该目标具有单调子模性，贪心算法提供(1-1/e)近似保证且存在匹配的难解性下界；所提规则能有效覆盖评估方向（梯度、对抗扰动、留出数据上的偏移）。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动的不确定性方向选择方法在理论上具有近似最优保证，在实践上提供可操作的覆盖性控制，为鲁棒优化中的不确定性建模提供了系统化框架。

Abstract: Robust machine learning and optimization rely on the uncertainty model choice. We investigate which uncertainty directions a model must cover when defined by a finite dictionary and a budget constraint. Selecting a subset forms an atomic uncertainty set with a closed form support function, yielding tractable robust programs for affine objectives. We propose a data driven selection rule based on a coverage objective over evaluation directions, including gradients, adversarial perturbations, or shifts observed on held out data. We prove this objective is monotone and submodular, supporting a greedy method with a $(1-1/e)$ approximation guarantee and a matching hardness barrier. We also provide a certificate bounding the loss from the selected subset and a radius calibration rule with out of sample control.

</details>


### [86] [Graph Structured Combinatorial Semi-Bandit with Nonlinear Reward Associations through Separable Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14650)
*Christoph Bauschmann,Setareh Maghsudi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于图因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程近似的通用自适应策略，用于在复杂非线性依赖关系下高效识别最优结构，并提供了亚线性时间和线性数据量的理论保证，通过合成与真实交通数据验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在互联数据中识别最优结构需要大量采样和计算资源，虽然学习信号依赖关系可提升效率，但非线性统计关系的普遍存在使得这一过程复杂度显著增加，亟需更高效的通用策略。

Method: 开发了结合图因果奖励建模、解析再生核方法和泰勒函数过程近似的通用自适应策略，建立了鲁棒性分析框架，涵盖噪声干扰、模型收敛和解空间不匹配等不确定性。

Result: 建立了随时间亚线性、随数据量线性的理论性能保证，在合成数据和真实交通数据集上均取得了有效的数值实验结果。

Conclusion: 该框架在极简条件假设下具有广泛适用性，所提出的多种变体可应对特定或扩展场景，为处理非线性依赖下的结构优化问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The identification of optimal structures within vast arrays of interconnected data necessitates significant sampling- and computational effort. Learning and leveraging underlying signal dependencies can improve efficiency and predictive capabilities considerably, but the ubiquity of nonlinear statistical relations amplifies the complexity of such undertakings. In this paper, we develop novel generic and adaptive strategies equipped with routines for graph-based causal reward modeling, analytic reproducing kernel methods, and Taylor approximation of functional processes. We establish theoretical performance guarantees sublinear in time and linear in data volume over time. Our analyses cover robustness to a multitude of uncertainties arising from noise interference, gradual model convergence, and solution space mismatch. The framework's general appeal is substantiated by a minimalistic set of conditions or reliance on prior estimates, while various outlined modifications address specific or extended settings. To demonstrate practical effectiveness, we conduct numerical experiments using both benchmarked synthetic and real-world transportation datasets.

</details>


### [87] [Beyond task performance: Decoding bioacoustic embeddings with speech features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14662)
*Ines Nolasco,Jules Cauzinille,Marius Miron,Gagan Narula,Milad Alizadeh,Emmanuel Fernandez,Matthieu Geist,Ellen Gilsenan-McMahon,Olivier Pietquin,Emmanuel Chemla,Sara Keen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过回归探针量化了六类生物声学预训练模型对88个eGeMAPS声学特征的编码能力，发现无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间，拼接嵌入效果最佳；响度特征易恢复（R²=0.76），基频最难（R²=0.33），并基于特征可恢复性与物种显著性提供了数据驱动的模型选择指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物声学中广泛使用预训练音频嵌入，但对其编码了哪些声学特征、哪些特征对特定任务有用知之甚少，这阻碍了模型透明性并限制了对稀有物种或数据稀缺领域的扩展。

Method: 使用88维eGeMAPS特征集，跨越六个分类群，应用线性和非线性回归探针（linear and nonlinear regression probes）来量化每个预训练模型捕捉到的声学属性。

Result: 1) 无免费午餐模式：无单一模型能覆盖全部特征空间；2) 拼接嵌入获得最高性能，表明模型间存在互补的声学空间覆盖；3) 响度特征编码最佳（R²=0.76），基频F0最难恢复（R²=0.33）。

Conclusion: 通过交叉参考特征可恢复性与每个物种的特征显著性（NMI），推导出数据驱动的生物声学模型选择指导原则。

Abstract: Pretrained audio embeddings are standard in bioacoustics, yet little is known about which acoustic features these models encode, nor which are useful for a given task. This hinders transparency and limits extension to rare species or data-scarce domains. Here we reveal which speech-like features are encoded in bioacoustic representations. Using the 88~eGeMAPS features across six taxonomic groups, we apply linear and nonlinear regression probes to quantify which acoustic properties each model captures. Results confirm a ``no free lunch'' pattern: no single model captures the full feature space. A concatenated embedding achieves the highest performance, suggesting complementary acoustic space coverage across models. Loudness features are best encoded ($R^2 = 0.76$) while F0 is hardest to recover ($R^2 = 0.33$). By cross-referencing recoverability with per-species feature salience (NMI), we derive data-driven model selection guidance for bioacoustics.

</details>


### [88] [When to Write and When to Suppress: Route-Specialized Dual Adapters for Memory-Assisted Knowledge Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14668)
*Yining Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出路由专用双适配器编辑器（RoSE），通过相关性路由器决定是否注入编辑记忆，分别使用编辑适配器和局部适配器处理编辑和非编辑提示，在三个基准上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑系统需要更新选定事实同时保持附近无关行为不变，核心设计问题不仅是如何写入编辑，还包括何时抑制编辑。

Method: 提出RoSE方法，包含一个相关性路由器决定提示是否接收编辑记忆，路由后的提示使用编辑适配器（偏好新对象），非路由提示使用局部适配器（保留或恢复原始对象偏好）。

Result: 在Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上，CF取得0.8180，ZSRE取得0.8946，MQuAKE取得0.9922的最优概率偏好准确率；在Qwen3-8B上趋势一致。路由器消融显示不同数据集最佳路由边界不同；组件消融表明增益主要来自分离编辑注入与离路抑制，而非单纯增加LoRA容量。

Conclusion: 分离编辑注入与离路抑制是提升知识编辑性能的关键，不同数据集需要适配不同的路由策略。

Abstract: Knowledge editing systems must update selected facts while preserving nearby but irrelevant behavior. This paper studies this problem in a memory-assisted setting where an edit memory is retrieved at inference time and a parameter-efficient adapter corrects the model's object preference. We argue that the central design question is not only how to write an edit, but also when to suppress it. We introduce \method{}, a route-specialized dual-adapter editor. A relevance router first decides whether a prompt should receive an edit memory. Routed prompts use an edit adapter trained to prefer the new object over the original object; unrouted non-direct prompts use a separate locality adapter trained to preserve or restore the original-object preference. We evaluate \method{} on three 1,000-case protocols, \cf{}, \zsre{}, and \mquake{}, under the same memory protocol and two 7B/8B base models. On Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, \method{} obtains the best overall probability-preference accuracy on all three benchmarks: 0.8180 on \cf{}, 0.8946 on \zsre{}, and 0.9922 on \mquake{}. The same trend holds on Qwen3-8B. Router ablations show that the relevant memory boundary differs across datasets: a lexical neural router is safest on \cf{}, while BGE embedding routing is better on \zsre{} and \mquake{}. Component and module ablations show that the gain mainly comes from separating edit injection from off-route suppression rather than from simply increasing LoRA capacity.

</details>


### [89] [Compressed Computation is (probably) not Computation in Superposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14673)
*Jai Bhagat,Sara Molas-Medina,Giorgi Giglemiani,Stefan Heimersheim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过实验证明压缩计算（CC）玩具模型并非叠加计算的有效实例，其性能提升主要源于残差流中的噪声混合效应而非真正的叠加计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Braun等人提出的压缩计算（CC）模型是否真正属于叠加计算（computation in superposition）的实例。

Method: 1) 分析模型通过噪声残差流混合输入，对应标签中非预期的混合矩阵；2) 将训练目标分解为ReLU项和混合项；3) 分析神经元方向与混合矩阵特征子空间的关系；4) 基于混合矩阵构建半非负矩阵分解（SNMF）基线模型。

Result: 1) 性能增益与混合矩阵的规模正相关，移除混合矩阵后性能消失；2) 学习到的神经元方向集中在混合矩阵前50个特征值对应的子空间；3) SNMF基线能复现定性损失曲线并优于此前基线，但未达到训练模型水平。

Conclusion: CC模型不适合作为叠加计算的玩具模型，其表现由混合效应主导而非真正的计算叠加。

Abstract: We study whether the Compressed Computation (CC) toy model (Braun et al., 2025) is an instance of computation in superposition. The CC model appears to compute 100 ReLU functions with just 50 neurons, achieving a better loss than expected from only representing 50 ReLU functions. We show that the model mixes inputs via its noisy residual stream, corresponding to an unintended mixing matrix in the labels. Splitting the training objective into the ReLU term and the mixing term, we find that performance gains scale with the magnitude of the mixing matrix and vanish when the matrix is removed. The learned neuron directions concentrate in the subspace associated with the top 50 eigenvalues of the mixing matrix, suggesting that the mixing term governs the solution. Finally, a semi-non-negative matrix factorization (SNMF) baseline derived solely from the mixing matrix reproduces the qualitative loss profile and improves on prior baselines, though it does not match the trained model. These results suggest CC is not a suitable toy model of computation in superposition.

</details>


### [90] [Optimal Hidden-Target Learning for Online Inventory Optimization on General Convex Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14679)
*Anthony Pineci,Yunzong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了在任意有界凸容量集上，通过维护在线学习器选择的隐藏目标并投影到当前可行集，可实现最优的在线库存优化。基于在线梯度下降的方法将已知遗憾界从逆需求概率平方根提升到逆需求概率线性根，并给出了匹配下界。该原理还首次为强凸损失提供了多对数遗憾保证，为一般凸容量集提供了动态遗憾保证。分析引入了范数对齐原理，将问题简化为标量队列控制，并得到实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线库存优化（OIO）是带有物理记忆的在线凸优化——库存结转使得可行动作集依赖于历史决策。现有方法在单一线性容量约束下表现良好，但对于一般凸容量集的遗憾界仍有改进空间。本文旨在证明一个简单原则（维护隐藏目标并投影到当前可行集）在一般有界凸容量集上的最优性，并据此改进现有遗憾界。

Method: 提出维护由在线学习器选择的隐藏目标，并实现其到当前可行订货-至-集的正交投影。基于在线梯度下降作为基学习器。引入范数对齐原理：将隐藏目标到可行集的距离（用与投影相同的范数度量）作为状态变量，该距离按路径演化为标量队列，目标移动为到达项，共同需求为服务项。将问题约简为一维队列控制。

Result: （1）将已知遗憾界从逆需求概率平方根（1/√p）改进为逆需求概率线性根（1/p），并给出匹配的Ω(1/p)下界；（2）首次为强凸损失给出多对数遗憾保证；（3）首次为一般凸容量集给出适应欧几里得路径变化的动态遗憾保证；（4）实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 维护隐藏目标并投影的简单原则对一般有界凸容量集上的在线库存优化是最优的。范数对齐原理有效解决了状态依赖性问题，将高维问题约简为一维队列控制，从而将理论保证扩展到更广泛的凸容量集，超越了先前基于乘积性方法的能力范围。

Abstract: Online inventory optimization (OIO) is online convex optimization with physical memory: inventory carryover makes the feasible action set depend on the past. A natural principle, used in stochastic inventory learning and recently in OIO under a single linear capacity constraint, is to maintain a hidden target chosen by an online learner and implement its projection onto the currently feasible order-up-to set. We prove that this simple principle is optimal for OIO on arbitrary bounded convex capacity sets. With online gradient descent as the base learner, the method improves the best known regret guarantee for OIO on general convex sets from inverse to inverse-square-root dependence on the common-demand probability, and we prove a matching lower bound. The same principle gives the first polylogarithmic regret guarantee for strongly convex losses and the first dynamic regret guarantee adapting to Euclidean path variation on general convex capacity sets.
  The analysis introduces a norm alignment principle: the right state variable is the distance from the hidden target to the feasible set, measured in the same norm as the projection. Under norm alignment, this distance evolves pathwise as a scalar queue, with target movement as arrival and common demand as service. This reduction to one-dimensional queue control resolves the state dependence and extends the guarantees to general convex capacity sets, beyond the reach of prior productwise approaches. Experiments on synthetic and real-world inventory data corroborate the theory.

</details>


### [91] [A Complexity Measure for Active Learning in Multi-group Mean Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14690)
*Abdellah Aznag,Rachel Cummings,Adam N. Elmachtoub*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多组均值估计d臂老虎机中的最大风险主动学习目标，开发了局部极小极大框架并证明了首个通用下界，将难度分解为预算项、异质性指数和方差局部曲率三个正交因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多组均值估计的主动学习场景中，现有方法缺乏对最坏情况不确定性（max-risk）目标的理论下界，无法系统理解学习困难度的来源。

Method: 提出局部极小极大框架；引入损失诱导的ℓ1决策空间几何和基于表示的问题生成器，将困难实例构造转化为显式随机矩阵计算。

Result: 证明了首个针对max-risk目标的下界，分离出预算项、异质性指数和方差局部曲率三个因素；对光滑类族，VLC可重新参数化为方差-费舍尔信息并得到闭式解；与最强上界对比显示在广泛场景下近最优（对数因子内），但在高异质性实例中存在系统性差距。

Conclusion: 建立了多组均值估计max-risk目标的极小极大下界理论，揭示了问题难度分解结构，为主动学习策略设计提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We study a \emph{max-risk} objective for active learning in a multi-group mean estimation $d$-armed bandits: a learner adaptively allocates a budget of $T$ samples across $d$ groups to minimize the worst-case uncertainty index $\max_{k\in[d]}σ_k^2/n_k$, where $σ_k$ is the standard deviation of the distribution of arm $d$, and $n_k$ is the number of times arm $d$ is sampled. We develop a local minimax framework and prove the first general lower bound for this objective, valid for any finite-variance hypothesis class. The bound separates difficulty into three orthogonal factors: a \emph{budget} term, a \emph{heteroscedasticity} index measuring how unevenly the uncertainty is spread across arms, and a model-dependent complexity measure, the \emph{Variance Local Curvature} ($\mathrm{VLC}$), which captures how much information a local change of variance creates inside the hypothesis class. For smooth classes, the $\mathrm{VLC}$ is a reparametrization of a variance--Fisher information, with closed-form values for common families. Benchmarking against the strongest available upper bound shows near-optimality up to logarithmic factors in broad regimes, and pinpoints a systematic gap in highly heterogeneous instances. Our proof introduces two key ingredients: a loss-induced $\ell_1$ geometry on the decision space, and a representation-based instance generator that reduces hard-instance construction to an explicit random matrix calculation.

</details>


### [92] [Persona-Pruner: Sculpting Lightweight Models for Role-Playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14695)
*Jinsu Kim,Jihoon Tack,Noah Lee,Jongheon Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单个描述中提取特定于角色身份的子网络，将通用大语言模型压缩为轻量级角色扮演模型，在保持角色扮演性能的同时显著降低计算成本，在RoleBench上相比最强基线将性能下降减少了93.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型作为角色扮演聊天机器人虽表现出色，但在需要大量NPC同时互动的实际应用场景中，为每个角色部署完整通用模型会导致计算成本过高。作者质疑是否每个角色都需要完整的通用模型，假设特定角色身份仅依赖模型总容量的一小部分。

Method: 提出Persona-Pruner框架，通过从单个角色描述中隔离出特定于角色的子网络，构建轻量级角色扮演模型。与朴素剪枝不同，该方法能区分冗余知识和必要角色特征。

Result: 在RoleBench上，Persona-Pruner比现有最先进的LLM剪枝技术更有效地保持角色扮演性能，在LLM-as-a-judge评分上相比最强基线将性能下降减少了93.8%，同时保持通用LLM能力。

Conclusion: Persona-Pruner能够有效提取角色特定的子网络，大幅降低计算开销的同时保持角色扮演质量和通用能力，代码已开源。

Abstract: Language Models (LMs) have shown remarkable potential as role-playing chatbots, delivering consistent, stylized interactions when given a specification of a character or user persona. However, applying these capabilities to real-world applications (e.g., ecosystems with numerous NPCs interacting simultaneously) exposes a critical inefficiency due to the excessive computational cost. In this paper, we question the necessity of dedicating a full, generalist model to a single persona, hypothesizing that a specific character identity relies on only a fraction of the model's total capacity. We observe that naively pruning LMs often severely degrades the role-playing performance for a specific persona; it does not distinguish between redundant knowledge and essential character traits. We propose Persona-Pruner, a framework that sculpts a lightweight role-playing model by isolating persona-specific sub-networks from a single description. Our experiments consistently show that Persona-Pruner preserves role-playing performance substantially more effectively than existing state-of-the-art LLM pruning techniques, reducing the performance drop from the dense model by up to 93.8% over the strongest baseline on RoleBench in LLM-as-a-judge score, while still maintaining general LLM capabilities. Code is available at https://github.com/jsu-kim/Persona-Pruner.

</details>

### [93] [Lattice Aggregation in Distributed Verification under Crash and Byzantine Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13954)
*Gilde Valeria Rodríguez,Borzoo Bonakdarpour,Armando Castañeda,Sergio Rajsbaum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出c-格点聚合问题，一种面向分布式验证的容错重构协议，在崩溃和拜占庭故障下实现异步进程对并发执行I的完整重建。通过冗余参数x刻画输入，给出了崩溃故障下x ≥ t + 1和拜占庭故障下x ≥ 2t + c的严格可行性阈值，并证明了这些界是紧的。此外，定义了全局依赖语言，证明监控系统的可靠性当且仅当c-格点聚合问题可解，首次完整刻画了拜占庭故障下的容错验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在异步分布式系统中，多个进程各自持有局部样本，需要协作重构完整的并发执行I，但面临崩溃和拜占庭故障。现有格点协议（Lattice Agreement）及其拜占庭变体无法直接解决这一问题，需要更强的容错重构机制来确保正确进程输出完整且可比较的执行结果。

Method: 将问题参数化为冗余参数x（I中每个元素至少出现在x个初始样本中），基于SCD-broadcast设计匹配算法实现上界，利用不可区分性论证推导下界，从而建立严格的可行性阈值。同时定义全局依赖语言类别，将监控系统的可靠性与c-格点聚合问题的可解性等价关联。

Result: 崩溃故障下，格点聚合可解当且仅当x ≥ t + 1；拜占庭故障下（t < n/3），c-格点聚合可解当且仅当x ≥ 2t + c。所有界均为紧的。全局依赖语言（包括共识、线性一致性、k-set agreement、领导者选举）的监控可靠性等价于c-格点聚合的可解性。

Conclusion: 首次完整刻画了异步拜占庭故障下分布式验证的容错可行性边界，为分布式监控系统的设计提供了理论基础和紧的冗余条件。

Abstract: We introduce c-Lattice Aggregation, a fault-tolerant reconstruction problem for distributed verification under crash and Byzantine failures. In our setting, n asynchronous processes supervise a concurrent execution I: each process holds a local sample, and must collaboratively reconstruct I from partial, potentially overlapping observations. A protocol solves c-Lattice Aggregation if at least c correct processes output the complete execution I, while all correct outputs are comparable and bounded by I. This strengthens Lattice Agreement [Attiya, Herlihy and Rachman, 1995] and Byzantine Lattice Agreement [Di Luna et al., 2020; Zheng and Garg, 2020].
  We parameterize inputs by a redundancy parameter x -- every element of I appears in at least x initial samples -- and establish tight feasibility thresholds. Under crash failures with at most t faulty processes, Lattice Aggregation is solvable if and only if x >= t + 1. Under Byzantine failures with t < n/3, c-Lattice Aggregation is solvable if and only if x >= 2t + c. All bounds are tight: we present matching algorithms based on SCD-broadcast [Imbs et al., 2018; Khanchandani and Wattenhofer, 2024] and indistinguishability-based lower bounds.
  Finally, we define globally dependent languages -- those for which no partial view can certify correctness, including consensus, linearizability, k-set agreement, and leader election -- and prove that soundness of any monitoring system is achievable if and only if c-Lattice Aggregation is solved, yielding the first complete characterization of fault-tolerant verification under Byzantine failures.

</details>


### [94] [STREAM: Multi-Tier LLM Inference Middleware with Dual-Channel HPC Token Streaming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13968)
*Anas Nassar,Steve Mohr,Leonard Apanasevich,Himanshu Sharma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: STREAM提出了一种三层级联路由架构（本地、HPC、云端），通过智能复杂度判别、双通道HPC流式传输、层级感知的上下文摘要和HPC-as-API代理模式，首次统一了三种推理环境，在1200查询基准上实现了85.1%的免费层保留率和亚秒级TTFT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型推理面临碎片化困境：本地模型免费且私密但受限于硬件资源，HPC集群虽强大但在防火墙后且仅支持批处理作业，商业云API收费高且存在数据留存政策。缺乏统一三种环境的系统。

Method: (1) 三层路由架构，包含本地LLM复杂度判断器；(2) 双通道HPC流式架构，将Globus Compute控制面与WebSocket中继数据面分离，实现防火墙穿透和AES-256-GCM加密；(3) 层级感知上下文摘要；(4) HPC-as-API代理模式，暴露兼容OpenAI的端点。

Result: Llama 3.2 3B在1200查询基准上达到85.1%免费层保留率；TTFT：本地0.26秒，HPC中继0.54秒（对比批处理模式11.40秒提升21.1倍），云端1.68秒。

Conclusion: STREAM成功统一了本地、HPC和云端三种推理环境，通过智能路由和流式架构显著降低了HPC推理延迟，使非专家用户也能便捷使用HPC资源，为敏感数据研究提供了兼顾性能、隐私和成本的有效方案。

Abstract: Researchers and practitioners working with large language models face a fragmented landscape: local models are free and private but hardware limits the model size and context windows a researcher can use; institutional HPC centers offer powerful GPU resources at no marginal cost and keep data within institutional boundaries, but operate behind firewalls and are designed for batch jobs rather than interactive use; commercial cloud APIs provide frontier-model quality on demand but impose significant cost and data retention policies unsuitable for sensitive research data. No existing system unifies all three. STREAM (Smart Tiered Routing Engine for AI Models) addresses this gap with four contributions: (1) a three-tier routing architecture combining local, HPC, and cloud inference with a local LLM-based complexity judge; (2) a dual-channel HPC streaming architecture that separates the Globus Compute control plane (authentication and job dispatch) from a WebSocket relay data plane (token delivery), enabling sub-second TTFT (0.54 s median, 21.1x over batch mode's 11.40 s) through institutional firewalls without VPN or firewall rule changes, with end-to-end AES-256-GCM encryption ensuring the relay operator cannot read token payloads; (3) tier-aware context summarization that prevents long conversations from forcing simple queries onto expensive tiers; and (4) an HPC-as-API proxy mode that exposes HPC inference as an OpenAI-compatible endpoint callable from any standard client with no HPC expertise, a deployment pattern made practical only by the sub-second TTFT of contribution (2). Llama 3.2 3B achieves 85.1% free-tier retention on a 1,200-query benchmark spanning ten domains. Measured TTFT: 0.26 s local, 0.54 s HPC (relay), 1.68 s cloud.

</details>


### [95] [VaultxGPU: GPU-Accelerated Blockchain Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14007)
*Samuel Taiwo Fatunmbi,Om Amit Gandhi,Luke Logan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出VaultxGPU，一种基于GPU加速的PoS绘图器，利用CUDA和SYCL将Blake3哈希管道卸载到GPU，在K=31参数下实现59.2倍加速（45.4秒 vs 2688秒），超越384线程CPU性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PoW共识机制能耗巨大（比特币年耗约150 TWh），PoS虽然通过存储替代计算降低了能耗，但绘图生成仍受限于CPU哈希吞吐量瓶颈，亟需更高效的加速方案。

Method: 在VaultX CPU绘图器基础上，将Blake3哈希管道通过自定义内核卸载到GPU；支持CUDA（NVIDIA）和SYCL（AMD/Intel）两种后端；将Table 1完全驻留GPU VRAM，并将排序和匹配阶段融合为单个内核以减少数据移动。

Result: SYCL GPU实现在K=31时实现59.2倍加速（45.4秒 vs 2688秒单线程基线），超越最佳384线程CPU配置；K值27-31的实验均验证了GPU加速的有效性。

Conclusion: GPU加速是超越CPU并行极限、扩展PoS绘图能力的正确方向。

Abstract: Blockchain consensus mechanisms based on Proof-of-Work consume significant energy, with Bitcoin alone estimated at approximately 150 TWh per year. Proof-of-Space reduces this cost by replacing repeated computation with storage, but plot generation remains bottlenecked by CPU hashing throughput. Prior work on VaultX demonstrated a high-performance CPU-based Proof-of-Space plotter using multi-threaded Blake3 hashing, achieving plotting speeds 4 to 50x faster than Chia depending on hardware configuration. In this paper, we present VaultxGPU, a GPU-accelerated extension of the VaultX plotter that offloads the Blake3 hashing pipeline to the GPU using custom kernels. We implement the plotter in both CUDA for NVIDIA hardware and SYCL for AMD and Intel GPUs, keeping Table 1 entirely in GPU VRAM and fusing the sort and match stages into a single kernel to minimize data movement. We evaluate VaultxGPU across K-values 27 through 31 against CPU baselines. Our SYCL GPU implementation achieves a 59.2x speedup over a single-threaded CPU baseline, completing a K=31 plot in 45.4 seconds compared to 2688 seconds, and outperforms even the best 384-thread CPU configuration. These results confirm that GPU acceleration is the correct direction for scaling Proof-of-Space plotting beyond what CPU parallelism can achieve.

</details>


### [96] [Extreme-Scale Atomistic Simulation of Real-Temperature Magnetic Skyrmion Dynamics by Coupled Spin-Lattice Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14073)
*Pin Chen,Cheng-bing Chen,Hai Liu,Yuewen Huang,Kangyou Zhong,Hai-Jun Zhao,Liu-Liu Han,Guixin Guo,Jiang Li,Dan Huang,Ben Xu,Yutong Lu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文通过将自旋约束的密度泛函理论训练的神经进化势与结构保持的自旋-晶格积分器结合在一个机器学习框架中，实现了在亿亿级超算上对1.34万亿原子和自旋的模拟，直接解析了真实温度下FeGe中螺旋-斯格明子转变的拓扑磁动力学，相比先前方法实现了七个数量级的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 功能材料中的真实温度拓扑磁动力学受晶格与自旋耦合演化支配，但在器件相关尺度上难以进行预测性模拟，亟需一种能够同时达到原子分辨率、显式晶格运动和微米尺度域的高效模拟方法。

Method: 结合自旋约束的密度泛函理论训练的神经进化势与结构保持的自旋-晶格积分器，形成统一机器学习框架；通过架构特定优化（内核融合、SVE2向量化、NUMA感知数据布局）实现加速；部署于LineShine亿亿级超算。

Result: 相比先前自旋感知方法实现七个数量级加速；在LineShine超算上扩展至1245万CPU核心，弱扩展效率89.7%；实现1.34万亿原子和等量自旋的模拟，双精度性能达48.5 PFLOPS；直接解析了真实温度下斯格明子成核与重组过程。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了耦合自旋-晶格拓扑磁动力学预测性模拟的新范式，使器件相关尺度上的真实温度模拟成为可能。

Abstract: Real-temperature topological magnetic dynamics in functional materials is governed by coupled lattice and spin evolution, yet remains inaccessible to predictive simulation at device-relevant scales. As a flagship example, thermally driven helix-to-skyrmion transformation in FeGe requires atomistic resolution, explicit lattice motion, and micrometer-scale domains to resolve device-scale topological texture formation. We combine a spin-constrained density-functional-theory-trained neuro-evolution potential with a structure-preserving spin-lattice integrator within one machine-learned framework. Architecture-specific optimizations, kernel fusion, SVE2 vectorization, and NUMA-aware data layout deliver a seven orders-of-magnitude speedup over prior spin-aware methods. Deployed on LineShine exascale supercomputer, the full application scales to 12.45 million CPU cores with 89.7% weak-scaling efficiency, enabling simulations of 1.34 trillion atoms and an equal number of spins while reaching 48.5 PFLOPS in double precision. The simulations directly resolve real-temperature skyrmion nucleation and reorganization at previously inaccessible scales, establishing a new regime for predictive simulation of coupled spin-lattice topological magnetic dynamics.

</details>


### [97] [On the Limits of Causal Observation in Shared-Memory Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14093)
*Gilde Valeria Rodríguez,Armando Castañeda,Miguel Piña*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文形式化了并发系统的因果可观测性问题（COP），证明了在操作边界上同时实现完备性和正确性的强一致解是不可能的，并揭示了埋点位置（内部/外部）决定可观测行为的核心原理。作者实现了三个非阻塞监控器对象，验证了位置决定行为、内部一致性不影响COP保证的理论，并在64核NUMA架构上实证了Striped在保持线性izability的同时达到与Collect相当的吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发系统中判定操作先后顺序是正确性推理的基础。现有方法在可观测边界上无法同时保证完备性（不漏真顺序）和正确性（不报假顺序），需要形式化该问题并探索根本原因与可行方案。

Method: 形式化定义了因果可观测问题（COP），证明强一致解不可达。提出埋点位置决定论：内部埋点得完备性，外部埋点得正确性，二者不可兼得。基于此设计了三个非阻塞因果监控器：FAInc（集中原子计数器）、Striped（分散计数器）、Collect（迭代寄存器快照），其中FAInc和Striped线性izable，Collect仅quiescently consistent。

Result: 理论证明强一致（同时完备且正确）解在可观测边界上不可实现；埋点位置独立于底层时间戳机制决定可观测行为。实验表明Striped在64核NUMA上匹配Collect吞吐量，同时保持线性izability，消除了FAInc在高线程数下的缓存争用瓶颈。

Conclusion: 并发监控中可观测行为由埋点位置决定而非内部一致性；Striped实现了高吞吐与强一致性的兼得，为实际系统设计提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Determining whether one concurrent operation completed before another began is a fundamental prerequisite for reasoning about the correctness of concurrent systems. We formalize this challenge as the Causal Observability Problem (COP): assign timestamps to the observable boundary events of a concurrent execution, invocations and responses, that faithfully reflect real-time operation order. A solution is complete if it never misses a genuine precedence, and sound if it never reports a spurious one. We prove that a strongly consistent solution, one that is simultaneously complete and sound, is unachievable at the observable boundary. We then show that the placement of instrumentation events relative to operation boundaries deterministically governs what a monitor can guarantee: internal placement yields completeness, external placement yields soundness, and neither achieves both. This dichotomy holds independently of the underlying timestamp mechanism. We instantiate this framework with three non-blocking implementations of a Causal Monitor object: FAInc (centralized atomic counter), Striped (decentralized counter), and Collect (iterative register snapshot). FAInc and Striped are linearizable; Collect is only quiescently consistent. Despite this internal consistency gap, we prove that all three provide identical COP guarantees: placement alone determines observable behavior. We validate these claims empirically on a 64-core NUMA architecture, showing that Striped matches Collect in throughput while preserving linearizability, resolving the cache-contention bottleneck of FAInc at high thread counts.

</details>


### [98] [Design Methodology and Performance Trade-offs Management for Distributed and Compound AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14350)
*Milos Gravara,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出从模型中心设计转向系统中心设计的复合AI系统方法论，通过工作流拓扑和配置选择两个维度组织设计空间，识别出八种设计模式，在三个案例研究中验证了该方法能够保持接近单体模型精度（差距2.5-4个百分点），同时降低延迟最高60%和成本最高71%，并指出通往自动发现与维护SLO合规的分布式AI系统面临五大开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模型中心的AI系统在运行时面临性能退化、成本上升等问题，因为单体模型在设计时固定了知识、对所有输入施加相同计算量、无法分解任务给专用组件，导致操作目标被单一设计选择所耦合，亟需从模型中心转向系统中心的设计范式。

Method: 提出一种设计方法论，沿工作流拓扑（workflow topology）和配置选择（configuration selection）两个维度组织设计空间，识别出八种设计模式，每种模式整合特定技术以解决单体部署的某一局限性，并通过三个案例研究进行验证。

Result: 复合AI系统配置在精度上接近单体模型（差距2.5-4个百分点），同时延迟降低最高60%，成本降低最高71%。模型选择与参数配置共同决定系统性能，但设计空间随工作流组合更多模式和组件呈组合爆炸式增长。

Conclusion: 识别出五大开放挑战，构成了从手动配置原型向自动发现与维护SLO合规的复合与分布式AI系统的路线图。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) systems must typically satisfy service-level objectives including accuracy, latency, and cost. The prevailing model-centric approaches select a monolithic model at design time and apply identical computation regardless of input difficulty, cannot decompose tasks across specialized components, and have knowledge that is fixed at training time. During runtime, this can lead to performance degradation and increasing costs. Because the model is the main design variable, it determines the majority of system behavior, coupling operational objectives to a single design-time choice. Addressing these limitations requires shifting from model-centric to system-centric design. Compound AI systems realize this shift by orchestrating multiple models, algorithms, and tools as distributed AI systems through explicit control logic. The performance of such systems depends on their workflow topology, the models assigned to each task, and the parameters governing runtime behavior. We present a design methodology that organizes this space along two dimensions, workflow topology and configuration selection, and identifies eight design patterns, each consolidating techniques to address a specific limitation of monolithic deployment. We validate our methodology through three case studies. Across our case studies, Compound AI configurations approach accuracy of monolithic models within 2.5 to 4 percentage points while reducing latency by up to 60% and cost by up to 71%. We show that model selection and parameter configuration jointly determine system performance, but the resulting design space grows combinatorially, as workflows compose more patterns and components. Thus, we identify five open challenges that define a roadmap from manually configured prototypes towards systems that automatically discover and maintain SLO-compliance in Compound and Distributed AI systems.

</details>


### [99] [PLAIground: SLO-Driven Runtime Model Selection for Compound AI Systems in the Edge-Cloud-Space Continuum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14356)
*Milos Gravara,Cynthia Marcelino,Andrija Stanisic,Stefan Nastic*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PLAIground是一个支持Compound AI系统运行时模型选择的框架，通过CAIM抽象和Pixie算法，在保证SLO合规的同时实现高达91.3%的准确率，相比固定模型策略显著降低成本和延迟违规。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式和复合AI框架原生不支持运行时模型选择，而Compound AI系统需要动态切换模型以满足准确性、延迟和成本等多维SLO约束。

Method: 提出Compoundable AI Model (CAIM)抽象，通过Task和Data Contracts解耦任务语义与AI模型实现；提出Pixie算法，一种SLO驱动的运行时模型选择算法，动态为每个任务选择最合适的模型。

Result: 在两个真实Compound AI工作流上，Pixie实现了高达91.3%的准确率，同时保持SLO合规；相比之下，固定模型策略的成本和延迟预算违规高达21倍，或准确率目标偏差达4%。

Conclusion: PLAIground通过运行时模型选择有效解决了Compound AI系统在多维SLO约束下的模型动态切换问题，在准确率、成本和延迟之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Applications in the 3D Computing Continuum, which unifies edge, cloud, and space, require combining multiple AI tasks such as object detection, time-series analytics, and natural language processing into Compound AI systems. These systems must satisfy stringent Service Level Objectives (SLOs) on accuracy, latency, and cost. A key mechanism for maintaining SLO compliance of Compound AI systems is runtime model selection, where AI models are dynamically switched for each workflow task. However, existing distributed and compound AI frameworks do not natively support runtime model selection. We present PLAIground, a framework that enables runtime model selection for Compound AI systems. PLAIground introduces Compoundable AI Model (CAIM) abstraction, which decouples task semantics from AI model implementations via Task and Data Contracts, enabling model switching without workflow changes. Additionally, PLAIground introduces Pixie, an SLO-driven runtime model selection algorithm, which dynamically selects the most suitable model for each task during execution. Our evaluation on two realistic Compound AI workflows demonstrates that Pixie achieves up to 91.3% accuracy while maintaining SLO compliance where fixed-model strategies either violate cost and latency budgets up to 21x or miss accuracy targets by 4%.

</details>


### [100] [Simulation-Based Performance Evaluation of Sharded Blockchain Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14394)
*Om Amit Gandhi,Ioan Raicu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文设计并实现了一个基于SimPy的离散事件模拟器，用于评估分片区块链架构在不同网络条件下的性能表现。实验表明，在256个分片下，本地数据中心环境可达160万TPS，全球广域网环境可达60万TPS，但分片数量超过一定阈值后，协调开销和网络延迟会导致收益递减。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公有区块链面临可扩展性瓶颈，单纯增大区块或缩短出块间隔无法根本解决问题，因此需要分片技术来并行处理交易，但缺乏可配置的模拟工具来系统评估分片架构的性能边界。

Method: 基于SimPy构建离散事件模拟器，对挖矿、验证、跨分片协调、区块传播、吞吐量、平均出块时间和通信开销等核心过程进行建模，支持在不同网络假设（本地数据中心/全球广域网）下进行参数化配置和实验。

Result: 256分片下本地数据中心环境吞吐量达160万TPS，全球广域网环境达60万TPS；但分片数继续增加后，协调流量、同步和网络开销开始占主导，性能提升呈现收益递减趋势。

Conclusion: 并行执行能显著提升分片区块链的吞吐量，但收益并非无限制；跨分片协调和网络传播开销会随分片数量增长而成为新的瓶颈，设计分片系统时需要权衡并行度与协调成本。

Abstract: Public blockchains continue to struggle with scalability because improving throughput is not as simple as increasing block size or reducing block interval. Larger blocks increase validation and transmission cost, while shorter intervals raise the likelihood of propagation delays, forks, and stale blocks. These limits motivate sharding, where transaction processing is divided across multiple parallel shard groups. In this work, we present a configurable SimPy-based discrete-event simulator for evaluating sharded blockchain architectures under controlled workload and network assumptions. The simulator models mining, verification, inter-shard coordination, block dissemination, measured throughput, average block time, and communication overhead. Our simulator achieves 1.6M TPS at 256 shards under a local datacenter-like setup and 0.6M TPS in a global WAN setup, showing strong throughput gains from parallel execution. However, the gains are not unbounded: beyond a certain number of shards, coordination traffic, synchronization, and network overhead begin to dominate, leading to diminishing returns.

</details>


### [101] [Simple-IT: Practical Low-Latency Signature-Free BFT Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14404)
*Qianyu Yu,Juan Villacis,Giuliano Losa,Zhuolun Xiang,Xuechao Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出Simple-IT，一种无签名拜占庭容错共识协议，在保持与现有易受量子攻击协议相当吞吐量的同时，实现了4个消息延迟的理论延迟（乐观路径仅3个），并通过实验验证了其实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算对依赖易受量子攻击签名方案（如Ed25519、BLS12-381）的拜占庭容错共识协议构成威胁。切换至后量子安全签名方案代价高昂，而无签名协议仅依赖廉价且抗量子的认证通道。本文目标是探究无签名BFT共识协议能否在性能（尤其是延迟）上与现有量子脆弱的最先进协议匹敌。

Method: 提出Simple-IT，一种基于领导者、无签名的BFT共识协议。其理论延迟为4个消息延迟（最优为3），乐观路径仅需3个。协议设计简洁，易于实现并支持推测性流水线等实用优化。

Result: 在地理分布式测试床上的实验表明，Simple-IT在吞吐量和延迟方面均与现有最先进的量子脆弱协议具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Simple-IT证明无签名BFT共识协议可以在实际部署中同时实现高吞吐量和低延迟，为量子安全共识提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in quantum computing pose a looming threat to most current Byzantine fault-tolerant (BFT) consensus protocols, which rely on quantum-vulnerable public-key signature schemes such as Ed25519 and BLS12-381. Instead of switching to much more expensive post-quantum secure signature schemes, an alternative is to use signature-free protocols, which rely only on cheap, post-quantum secure authenticated channels.
  In this paper, we ask whether signature-free BFT consensus protocols can match the performance of current state-of-the-art, quantum-vulnerable BFT consensus protocols. While previous work on the Sailfish++ protocol showed that state-of-the-art throughput is attainable signature-free, the question of latency is still open. Several recent signature-free protocols have low latency in theory, but they are all very intricate, and no practical implementation has so far been presented. In this work, we propose Simple-IT, a new leader-based, signature-free BFT consensus protocol that achieves a theoretical latency of 4 message delays (one more than the optimum), and only 3 on its optimistic path. Crucially, Simple-IT is simple enough to be amenable to implementation and to practical optimizations such as speculative pipelining, and, as we show experimentally in a geo-distributed testbed, it achieves both throughput and latency competitive with state-of-the-art quantum-vulnerable protocols.

</details>


### [102] [parRSB: Exascale Spectral Element Mesh Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.14659)
*Thilina Ratnayaka,Paul Fischer*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了parRSB——一种基于递归谱二分法（RSB）的并行高可扩展图划分器，专为谱单元网格设计，能产生高质量划分。parRSB在输入网格的对偶图上实现RSB算法，利用Fiedler向量（拉普拉斯矩阵最小非零特征值对应的特征向量）进行划分决策，并最小化分区间的通信量。实现了Lanczos和共轭梯度逆迭代两种数值方法计算Fiedler向量。在Summit和Frontier超级计算机上展示了划分质量和可扩展性，并介绍了加速划分过程的优化结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对谱单元网格的高质量划分需求，开发一种并行、高可扩展的图划分工具，以最小化分区间的通信开销。

Method: 基于递归谱二分法（RSB），在对偶图上计算Fiedler向量进行二分划分，迭代递归至目标分区数；实现了Lanczos方法和共轭梯度逆迭代两种特征向量计算方法。

Result: 在Summit和Frontier超级计算机上验证了parRSB的划分质量和并行可扩展性，并展示了多项优化对划分效率的提升效果。

Conclusion: parRSB是一种高效、可扩展的并行图划分器，适用于大规模谱单元网格的划分任务，其基于Fiedler向量的RSB方法能有效降低通信开销。

Abstract: We introduce parRSB - a parallel, highly scalable graph partitioner for spectral element meshes that produce high quality partitions. parRSB is based on Recursive Spectral Bisection (RSB) algorithm implemented on the dual graph of the input mesh. RSB uses the Fiedler vector, which is the eigenvector associated with the smallest non-zero eigenvalue of the Laplacian matrix of the dual graph for making partitioning decisions and tries to minimize the communication volume between the partitions. We implemented two numerical methods: Lanczos, and Inverse iteration using Conjugate Gradient method to compute the Fiedler vector. We present partitioning results using parRSB on Summit and Frontier supercomputers at Oak Ridge National Laboratory to illustrate the quality of the partitions produced by parRSB and the scalability of our implementation. We also present results for some of the optimizations we did to speed up the partitioning process.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [103] [A Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL)-Based Transformer Method for Solving the Open Shop Scheduling Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13682)
*Faezeh Ardali,Mwembezi A. Nyelele,Gerald M. Knapp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Transformer编码器-解码器架构的开放车间调度问题（OSSP）调度策略，仅以加工时间矩阵为输入，在Taillard小规模基准实例上训练（4x4至10x10），生成的调度方案的makespan通常接近最佳已知值的15-30%以内。将该策略直接（无需重新训练）应用于40x40至100x00的大规模随机实例，其平均gap为12.89-15.12%，表现优于SPT和LPT，与EST方法竞争相当，表明在小规模实例上训练的Transformer策略可以有效泛化到大规模问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OSSP在工业和服务场景中广泛存在，但随着作业和机器数量增加，精确方法计算复杂度过高，经典调度规则和元启发式方法在大规模问题中需要大量调参才能保证解质量，因此亟需一种轻量、无需特征工程且具有良好泛化能力的学习型调度方法。

Method: 采用Transformer编码器-解码器架构，结合多头注意力机制；模型输入仅为加工时间矩阵；在Taillard基准实例（4x4, 5x5, 7x7, 10x10）上训练；训练完成后直接迁移至大规模随机实例（40x40至100x100），不再进行微调或重新训练。

Result: 在小规模Taillard实例上，模型生成的makespan通常位于最佳已知值的15-30%以内；在大规模随机实例上，平均gap为12.89-15.12%（相对于标准下界）；与经典规则相比，显著优于SPT和LPT，与EST方法差距较小，具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 在小规模OSSP实例上训练的Transformer调度策略能够有效泛化到更大规模的问题，提供了一种无需复杂特征工程、基于学习的替代方案，可媲美甚至超越经典调度规则。

Abstract: The open shop scheduling problem (OSSP) arises in many industrial and service settings but remains computationally challenging as the number of jobs and machines increases. While exact methods quickly become intractable, classical dispatching rules and metaheuristics may require substantial tuning to maintain solution quality at large scales. This study develops a Transformer-based scheduling policy for OSSP using an encoder-decoder architecture with multi-head attention. The model is trained on Taillard benchmark instances (4x4, 5x5, 7x7, and 10x10) using only the processing-time matrix as input and produces feasible schedules with makespans typically within 15-30% of best-known values. To evaluate scalability, the trained policy is applied without retraining to randomly generated instances from 40x40 to 100x100 and compared against classical dispatching heuristics, including SPT, LPT, MWKR, and EST. Across these large instances, the Transformer achieved average gaps of 12.89-15.12% relative to a standard lower bound. Compared with EST, the Transformer remained competitive, typically within a modest margin, while substantially outperforming SPT and LPT. These results indicate that a Transformer policy trained on small OSSP instances can generalize to substantially larger problems and provide a feature-light, learning-based alternative to classical dispatching rules.

</details>


### [104] [UP-NRPA: User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation for Planning with Large Language Models in Goal-oriented Dialogue Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13683)
*Hui Wang,Fafa Zhang,Meng Liu,Xiangyu Chen,Chaoxu Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于用户画像的嵌套策略自适应框架（UP-NRPA），利用大语言模型实现无需离线训练的对话策略动态定制，在协作与非协作对话基准测试中达到100%任务成功率，并在谈判任务中使销售/清单比提升56.41%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话策略规划方法难以动态适应多样化的用户特征，传统方法依赖模型训练和离线强化学习策略模型，无法实现针对不同用户的实时自适应。

Method: 提出UP-NRPA在线框架，利用大语言模型，通过用户画像映射用户个性、偏好和目标，结合实时用户反馈，通过自适应机制动态调整对话策略，无需离线强化学习。

Result: 在协作与非协作对话基准中，多个对话任务达到100%成功率；在谈判任务中，销售/清单比（SL）提升56.41%。

Conclusion: UP-NRPA无需训练机制即可适应多样化的用户需求，使对话系统能够根据用户特征进行自适应调整。

Abstract: To address the challenge that current dialogue policy planning methods struggle to dynamically adapt to diverse user characteristics, this paper proposes a User Portrait based Nested Rollout Policy Adaptation (UP-NRPA) online framework with Large Language Models. In contrast to conventional approaches dependent on model training and require offline reinforcement learning policy models for user groups, UP-NRPA enables dynamic customization of dialogue strategies through an adaptive mechanism. This is achieved by leveraging real-time user feedback alongside personality, preferences, and objectives mapped from the current user portrait, thereby adapting to user characteristics without offline reinforcement learning. In collaborative and non-collaborative dialogue benchmarks, UP-NRPA demonstrated considerable benefits, achieving an impressive 100% success rate in multiple dialogue tasks. Particularly in negotiation tasks, the sale-to-list ratio (SL) increased by 56.41%. This demonstrates that UP-NRPA can adapt to diverse user needs without requiring a training mechanism, enabling the dialogue system to adapt to user characteristics.

</details>


### [105] [History of the Muddy Children Puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13703)
*Hans van Ditmarsch*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文追溯了“泥孩子谜题”的起源（最早可追溯到19世纪），并提出了一个涉及自我指涉的新变种——帽子谜题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于“泥孩子谜题”的首次提出者不明确，本文旨在通过梳理过去两个世纪的逻辑与文学出版物，厘清其起源。

Method: 通过文献追溯（逻辑与文学出版物）考察谜题的历史演变，并构造一个新的自我指涉帽子谜题变种。

Result: 发现了该谜题更早的起源线索，并成功设计了一个涉及自我指涉的新型帽子谜题。

Conclusion: 该谜题的历史比通常认为的更久远，且自我指涉变种为认知逻辑提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: The Muddy Children Puzzle is a puzzle about knowledge and ignorance that has been inspiring for the development of epistemic logic. Who came up with it first? This is unclear. We trace the origin of the Muddy Children Puzzle through logical and literary publications over the past two centuries. The puzzle inspired a numerous variations such as involving numbers or coloured hats. We also present a novel hats puzzle involving self-reference.

</details>


### [106] [Orchestra-o1: Omnimodal Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13707)
*Fan Zhang,Vireo Zhang,Shengju Qian,Haoxuan Li,Hao Wu,Jinyang Wu,Donghao Zhou,Zhihong Zhu,Zheng Lian,Xin Wang,Pheng-Ann Heng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Orchestra-o1，一个全模态智能体编排框架，通过统一编排机制支持模态感知的任务分解、在线子智能体专业化和并行子任务执行，在全模态场景下超越现有方法10.3%的准确率，并引入DA-GRPO强化学习方法训练出性能最优的8B开源全模态智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体编排框架仅支持有限模态类型，无法泛化到异构模态共存并交互的复杂全模态场景，亟需一个能够统一理解和协调文本、图像、音频、视频等多模态输入的编排框架。

Method: 提出Orchestra-o1框架，包含统一编排机制、模态感知的任务分解、在线子智能体专业化和并行子任务执行；并引入决策对齐的群体相对策略优化（DA-GRPO）进行强化学习训练。

Result: 在OmniGAIA基准上超越第二名方法10.3%的准确率；Orchestra-o1-8B在开源全模态智能体中达到最优性能。

Conclusion: Orchestra-o1及其训练方法DA-GRPO能够有效解决全模态场景下的智能体编排问题，实现高效的跨模态协作。

Abstract: The recent success of agent swarms has shifted the paradigm of large language model (LLM)-based agents from single-agent workflows to multi-agent systems, highlighting the importance of agent orchestration for task decomposition and collaboration. However, existing orchestration frameworks are limited to a narrow set of modalities and struggle to generalize to more complex settings where heterogeneous modalities coexist and interact. This limitation becomes particularly pronounced in omnimodal scenarios, where tasks require the unified understanding and coordination of diverse inputs such as text, image, audio, and video. In this work, we propose Orchestra-o1, an omnimodal agent orchestration framework designed to support efficient agent collaboration across multiple modalities. Orchestra-o1 introduces a unified orchestration mechanism that enables modality-aware task decomposition, online sub-agent specialization, and parallel sub-task execution. This scalable design allows agent systems to effectively tackle complex real-world tasks involving heterogeneous information sources, surpassing the second-best approach by 10.3% accuracy on the OmniGAIA benchmark. Furthermore, we introduce decision-aligned group relative policy optimization (DA-GRPO), an efficient agentic reinforcement learning approach for training Orchestra-o1-8B, which also achieves state-of-the-art performance against all existing open-source omnimodal agents.

</details>


### [107] [Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution Makes Better Deep Researcher](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13710)
*Hongming Piao,Chi Liu,Mengzhuo Chen,Yan Shu,Derek Li,Ying Wei,Bryan Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出混合开放式三元进化（HOTE）框架，通过混合模式强化学习实现提议者、求解者和评判者的协同进化，在长程深度研究基准上，8B模型超越静态开源8-32B模型及现有深度研究方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度研究受限于静态参数化能力，而智能体进化仅在可验证任务上有效，两者之间存在鸿沟，亟需连接开放研究任务与自主进化。

Method: 提出HOTE框架，利用混合模式强化学习促进提议者（提出研究问题）、求解者（搜索整合信息）和评判者（评估结果）基于网络规模知识协同进化。

Result: 在三个长程深度研究基准上，8B模型超越最强静态开源8-32B模型以及现有深度研究训练方法，且时间开销更少。三个模块的进化均不可或缺。

Conclusion: HOTE框架有效桥接了深度研究与智能体进化两大任务，为开放环境下的自主进化智能体提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep research and agent evolution serve as de-facto tasks for AI agents in real-world applications toward artificial general intelligence. The former enables autonomous retrieval and integration of information in open-ended environments to tackle open-ended research tasks, yet it is constrained by the static parametric deep research capabilities of agent systems. The latter allows agents to autonomously interact with the environment to gain experiences that evolve model capabilities. However, its effectiveness has been widely validated only on verifiable tasks with standard answers, leaving a gap with open-ended research tasks. To bridge these two critical tasks, we propose the Hybrid Open-Ended Tri-Evolution (HOTE) framework, which leverages hybrid-mode reinforcement learning to facilitate the collaborative evolution of a proposer, solver and judge based on web-scale knowledge, moving toward autonomous evolving agents in open-ended tasks and environments. Extensive experiments on three long-form deep research benchmarks demonstrate that the 8B model trained via HOTE surpasses the strongest static open 8-32B models as well as those trained by state-of-the-art deep research training methods with less time overhead, and further verify that the evolution of all three modules in HOTE is indispensable.

</details>


### [108] [WorkBench Revisited: Workplace Agents Two Years On](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13715)
*Olly Styles*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: WorkBench基准测试显示，从2024年3月到2026年6月，最佳智能体任务完成率从43%提升至89%，有害行为发生率从26%降至2.5%。能力与安全性呈正相关，而非权衡；前沿模型虽消除了多类错误，但仍存在导致不可逆损害的基本失误；开源模型大幅降低了性能成本，前沿模型成本保持稳定。我们发布了更新版基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新评估前沿智能体在WorkBench上的进展，考察能力提升与安全性之间的动态关系，并分析开源模型对成本结构的影响。

Method: 对比分析2024年3月（GPT-4）与2026年6月（Claude Opus 4.8）在WorkBench上的任务完成率和非故意有害行为发生率，分类错误类型，并比较开源与前沿模型的成本。

Result: 最佳智能体任务完成率从43%提升至89%，有害行为发生率从26%降至2.5%；能力与安全性正相关；多类错误被消除但仍有基本失误；开源模型以更低成本达到了此前仅专有模型可及的性能水平。

Conclusion: 前沿智能体两年半来在能力与安全性上取得显著进步，两者非权衡关系；但基本错误仍有残留，开源模型的崛起正在改变成本-性能格局。

Abstract: The best agent on WorkBench in March 2024, GPT-4, completed 43% of tasks and took an unintended harmful action, such as emailing the wrong person, on 26% of them. We re-visit the benchmark in June 2026 and find that the best agent to date, Claude Opus 4.8, completes 89% and takes an unintended harmful action on 2.5%. Aside from this considerable progress in frontier agent performance, three things stand out. First, capability and safety go together on WorkBench rather than trade off, so the models that finish the most tasks also do the least unintended damage. Second, while several classes of error have been totally eliminated, frontier models still make some basic mistakes that occasionally result in irreversible harm, such as sending an email to the wrong person. Third, the rise of open-weight models has drastically lowered costs for a performance level that was previously only accessible to proprietary models, while frontier costs have stayed relatively stable. We release an updated version of the benchmark with data and code quality improvements, new model scores, and analysis of agent progress on WorkBench since 2024.

</details>


### [109] [Refusal Beyond a Single Direction: A Preliminary Comparison of Diff-in-Means and INLP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13720)
*Elisabetta Rocchetti,Alfio Ferrara*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对比了两种干预方法（DiM和INLP）在安全微调聊天模型中拒绝机制的效果，发现INLP反事实翻转与DiM方向消融在抑制拒绝行为上效果相当，而零空间投影效果较弱。限制INLP到主要方向可在保持低困惑度的情况下实现可调控制。几何分析表明两种INLP干预会将激活投射到截然不同的区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨能否用更丰富的参数化方法（INLP）替代简单的DiM方法来实现对模型拒绝行为的调控，并研究不同干预方法的几何特性差异。

Method: 在五个开源对话模型上，比较了DiM（激活添加和方向消融）与INLP（零空间投影和反事实翻转）四种干预方法，并分析了INLP子空间主导方向的影响及激活空间的几何变化。

Result: 1) INLP反事实翻转与DiM方向消融在拒绝抑制上效果相当；2) 零空间投影效果始终较弱；3) 限制INLP到主导方向可在近基线困惑度下保持抑制效果；4) 零空间投影将激活压缩到有害与无害簇之间，反事实翻转则将激活移动到相反簇。

Conclusion: INLP可作为DiM的有效替代方案，其丰富参数化能提供更精细的控制；模型对"概念缺失"与"概念对立"的编码存在本质差异，值得进一步研究。

Abstract: Arditi et al. (2024) has shown that refusal in safety fine-tuned chat models is mediated by a single linear direction in the residual stream, recoverable by a difference-in-means (DiM) of harmful and harmless activations. We compare DiM-based interventions (activation addition and directional ablation) with two interventions derived from Iterative Nullspace Projection (INLP) -- nullspace projection and counterfactual flipping -- on five open-weight chat models, asking whether INLP can match DiM at steering refusal and whether its richer parameterisation yields more tweakable interventions. INLP counterfactual flipping is competitive with DiM directional ablation on refusal suppression, while nullspace projection is consistently weaker. Restricting INLP to the leading directions of the extracted subspace preserves most of the suppression effect at near-baseline perplexity, giving a tunable capability. Geometrically, the two INLP interventions land in qualitatively different regions of activation space: nullspace projection collapses transformed activations \emph{between} the harmful and harmless clusters, while counterfactual flipping moves them into the opposite cluster, suggesting that the model encodes the absence of a concept differently from its opposite -- an intriguing distinction that warrants further investigation in future work.

</details>


### [110] [YeasierAgent: Agentic Social Sandbox as a Canvas for Intent-Driven Creation of Platform-Agnostic Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13722)
*Jory He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: YeasierAgent提出了一种基于共生智能体、叙事世界和场景感知交互的新型应用构建范式，通过将应用重新定义为用户、智能体和世界之间的协作空间，实现跨平台快速构建智能体原生应用，并统一了情感陪伴与实用工具执行功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战传统设备耦合的软件模型，解决现有应用构建方式中图形布局固定、跨平台困难的问题，同时弥合智能体在情感陪伴与工具执行之间的割裂。

Method: 提出平台无关的交互单元（智能体、场景、对话）替代固定图形布局；通过自动化生成、用户创建世界和空间多智能体协作，构建共生智能体原生应用。

Result: 实现了跨平台的智能体原生应用快速构建，并在单一体验沙盒中统一了智能体的情感陪伴与实用工具执行功能。

Conclusion: 形式化了共生智能体原生应用这一类别，展示了从孤立的、面向工具的聊天机器人向 cohesive 的、社会嵌入的计算环境的转变。

Abstract: This paper introduces YeasierAgent, an application-building paradigm based on symbiotic agents, narrative worlds, and scene-aware interaction. It challenges the conventional device-coupled model of software by redefining applications as collaborative spaces among users, agents, and worlds. We present a system architecture that achieves two primary contributions: (1) enabling the rapid, cross-platform construction of agent-native applications by utilizing platform-agnostic interactive units (agents, scenes, dialogue) rather than fixed graphical layouts; and (2) unifying the emotional companionship and practical tool execution attributes of intelligent agents within a single experiential sandbox. By integrating automated generation, user-created worlds, and spatial multi-agent collaboration, YeasierAgent formalizes the category of Symbiotic Agent-Native Applications, demonstrating a shift from isolated, tool-specific chatbots toward cohesive, socially embedded computational environments.

</details>


### [111] [TwinBI: An Agentic Digital Twin for Efficient Augmented Interactions with Business Intelligence Dashboards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13731)
*Jisoo Jang Wen-Syan Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TwinBI 是一个基于数字孪生的智能体框架，通过将 LLM 驱动助手与可执行的 BI 仪表盘状态耦合，实现了多步分析中对话交互与仪表盘操作的一致性。在 A/B 基准测试中，精确匹配准确率从 43.3% 提升至 63.3%，超时率从 40.0% 降至 10.0%；用户研究也验证了其有效性与可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在商业智能分析中，用户频繁切换仪表盘直接操作与自然语言查询，这两种模式在多步分析过程中容易失去同步，难以在过滤器、层级、指标和图表上下文中保持一致的语义状态。

Method: TwinBI 提出了一种智能体数字孪生框架，核心包括：(1) 基于 LLM 的智能体系统与可执行的 BI 仪表盘状态耦合；(2) 通过统一的交互日志重建共享分析状态，实现对话交互、仪表盘操作、语义锚定和溯源追踪的统一；(3) 暴露模式视图、SQL、日志及 /insights 命令等构件，用于基于状态的分析摘要生成。

Result: 在 A/B 基准测试中，相比纯仪表盘模式，TwinBI 将精确匹配准确率从 43.3% 提升至 63.3%，部分匹配准确率从 48.3% 提升至 70.8%，超时率从 40.0% 降低至 10.0%。用户研究表明，参与者受益于仪表盘与聊天集成的工作流，任务准确率高，工作负载适中，对状态感知交互机制的评分良好。

Conclusion: TwinBI 通过将可见的仪表盘状态转化为更丰富的可操作上下文，同时提升了智能体层面的分析可靠性和用户层面的分析支持能力。

Abstract: Business intelligence (BI) increasingly combines dashboard interaction with LLM-based assistance, but these two modes often fall out of sync during multi-step analysis. As users switch between direct dashboard manipulation and natural-language queries, it becomes difficult to preserve a consistent analytical state across filters, hierarchies, metrics, and chart context. We present TwinBI, an agentic digital-twin framework that couples an LLM-based agent system with an executable BI dashboard state. TwinBI unifies conversational interaction, dashboard manipulation, semantic grounding, and provenance tracking through a shared analytical state reconstructed from a unified interaction log. It also exposes artifacts such as schema views, SQL, logs, and an /insights command for state-grounded analytical summaries. We evaluate TwinBI in two complementary ways. In a controlled A/B benchmark with the same backbone agent, TwinBI improves exact-match accuracy from 43.3% to 63.3%, partial-credit accuracy from 48.3% to 70.8%, and substantially reduces timeout rate from 40.0% to 10.0% relative to Dashboard alone. In a usability study, participants benefited from the integrated dashboard-and-chat workflow, with high task accuracy, moderate workload, and favorable ratings for state-aware interaction mechanisms. These results suggest that TwinBI improves both agent-level analytical reliability and user-facing analytical support by turning visible dashboard state into richer actionable context. Our dataset and source code are available at: https://github.com/simonjisu/TwinBI

</details>


### [112] [When Sample Selection Bias Precipitates Model Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13732)
*Xinbao Qiao,Xianglong Du,Wei Liu,Jingqi Zhang,Peihua Mai,Meng Zhang,Yan Pang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在低资源场景下，基于本地碎片化参考分布的数据选择不仅无法防止模型坍塌，反而会加速坍塌并导致多样性呈幂律衰减；通过多数据孤岛构建Wasserstein代理参考分布可在不共享原始数据的情况下缓解该问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 递归使用合成数据训练虽能缓解数据稀缺，但存在模型坍塌风险（分布尾部被侵蚀、输出同质化）。数据选择被视为补救措施，但其可靠性严重依赖于验证器所使用的参考分布。在低资源验证场景下（如医疗联盟或金融机构的数据孤岛），每个验证器只能观测到目标流形的一个小碎片化且有偏切片，此时选择本身也会产生偏差，反而加速坍塌。

Method: 理论证明：证明孤岛式选择加速模型坍塌并导致多样性呈幂律衰减。提出缓解方法：在不共享原始数据的前提下，从多个数据孤岛构建Wasserstein代理参考分布，作为全局参考的近似。
